Question 1 characterspoiler arc 0 bothmultiple choice

Which character is the apothecary sold into the palace as a servant?

  • A. Maomao right answer
  • B. Xiaolan
  • C. Lihua
  • D. Lishu
  • C3 apothecary-diaries-cast-list-015 is draft: only verified facts may be cited

Question source: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_The_Apothecary_Diaries_characters

Cited facts

apothecary-diaries-cast-list-015 draftspoiler arc 0 bothmembers

series of apothecary-diaries, field characters

Value: Maomao (猫猫): the apothecary sold into the palace as a servant; Jinshi (壬氏): the influential official of the rear palace; Gaoshun (高順): Jinshi's loyal attendant; Gyokuyō (玉葉): the Precious Consort, the emperor's favorite; Lihua (梨花): the Wise Consort; Lishu (里樹): the Virtuous Consort; Ah-Duo (阿多): the Pure Consort; Lakan (羅漢): the monocle-wearing military tactician; Lihaku (李白): the soldier who gives Maomao a hairpin; Xiaolan (小蘭): Maomao's friend among the palace servants; Basen (馬閃): Gaoshun's second son and an attendant of Jinshi; Luomen (羅門): Maomao's adoptive father

Text: The twelve characters of this quiz run from Maomao and Jinshi to the four high-ranking consorts and the people around them.

Stored source text: The freckled adopted daughter of an apothecary and doctor named Luomen who lives in the red-light district of the capital city.
